CREATE TABLE [Flights] (
    [Id] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [FlightNumber] nvarchar(20) NOT NULL,
    [Source] nvarchar(50) NOT NULL,
    [Destination] nvarchar(50) NOT NULL,
    [DepartureTime] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [ArrivalTime] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [Gate] nvarchar(10) NOT NULL,
    [Aircraft] nvarchar(50) NOT NULL,
    [Status] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [TotalSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [AvailableSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [EconomySeats] int NOT NULL,
    [BusinessSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [FirstSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [CrewAssignment] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [EconomyPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [BusinessPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [FirstClassPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [CreatedAt] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [UpdatedAt] datetime2 NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_Flights] PRIMARY KEY ([Id])
);

CREATE TABLE [FlightSchedules] (
    [Id] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [FlightId] int NOT NULL,
    [DepartureTime] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [ArrivalTime] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [Gate] nvarchar(10) NOT NULL,
    [Status] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [TotalSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [AvailableSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [EconomySeats] int NOT NULL,
    [BusinessSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [FirstSeats] int NOT NULL,
    [EconomyPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [BusinessPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [FirstClassPrice] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [CreatedAt] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [UpdatedAt] datetime2 NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_FlightSchedules] PRIMARY KEY ([Id]),
    CONSTRAINT [FK_FlightSchedules_Flights_FlightId] FOREIGN KEY ([FlightId]) REFERENCES [Flights] ([Id]) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE [Bookings] (
    [Id] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [UserId] int NOT NULL,
    [UserEmail] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [UserName] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [FlightId] int NOT NULL,
    [ScheduleId] int NULL,
    [SeatClass] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [BaggageWeight] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [PNR] nvarchar(10) NOT NULL,
    [Status] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [PaymentStatus] nvarchar(max) NOT NULL,
    [TotalPassengers] int NOT NULL,
    [ConfirmedPassengers] int NOT NULL,
    [CancelledPassengers] int NOT NULL,
    [TotalAmount] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [CreatedAt] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [UpdatedAt] datetime2 NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_Bookings] PRIMARY KEY ([Id]),
    CONSTRAINT [FK_Bookings_FlightSchedules_ScheduleId] FOREIGN KEY ([ScheduleId]) REFERENCES [FlightSchedules] ([Id]) ON DELETE NO ACTION,
    CONSTRAINT [FK_Bookings_Flights_FlightId] FOREIGN KEY ([FlightId]) REFERENCES [Flights] ([Id]) ON DELETE NO ACTION
);

OUTPUT INSERTED.[Id], i._Position;
